- **evm-deployment**: Deploy Sablier EVM contracts (Comptroller, ERC20 Faucet, Flow, Lockup, Airdrops) with full workflow automation. - **evm-invariant-scan**: Autonomous EVM/Solidity security review. Triggers on 'evm scan', 'pre-audit', 'invariant scan', 'solidity audit', 'entry points', 'access control map', 'audit prep', 'find invariants'. - **evm-rpc**: Call Ethereum and EVM chains from IC canisters (Rust) via the EVM RPC canister using the evm_rpc_client crate.
